# The cutover's test disposition (#190, W6) — every case of every migration-bound module

> *Generated for slice 8's cutover (#190), 2026-10-29: the record ruling 4 (§0.3) requires — the
> 59 migration-bound modules of the inventory (`R10-W6-MIGRATION-INVENTORY.md` §6.5) and the
> collateral modules that also read a deleted migration, one row per test case, each with its fate.*
> Frozen history once merged; the living proof is the suite itself.

**The ruling (2026-10-29, point 4).** A case pinning a database rule's behaviour survives, re-anchored
on the fresh initial set's rules migrations. A case whose subject is solely historical migration
behaviour — a backfill, a guard, a rename operation, an app-label move, an executor round trip through
a deleted node — retires, naming the subject it pinned. A case that ALSO pinned a continuing runtime,
public or database invariant keeps that invariant in an end-state test before its migration half goes.
No case retires merely because its migration disappeared. A rule's reverse is proven once, centrally,
by `ubb-platform/apps/platform/tests/test_the_database_rules_are_carried.py::TheRulesMigrationsReverseTest`.

## The four passes' notes

### usage

- **Counts** (one row per pre-window case, 334 across 17 modules; the 13 cases of the new module are counted once, as `moved`): kept 254 · re-anchored 17 · moved 18 (5 to the central reverse, 13 to `test_the_usage_record_is_the_posting.py`) · retired 45. 334 − 45 − 18 + 13 = 284 = what collects now (267 in the 16 usage modules + 17 in `test_analytics_scale.py`). Modules deleted 1 (`test_posting_rename.py`); created 1 (`test_the_usage_record_is_the_posting.py`). `apps/metering/usage/tests/` + `test_analytics_scale.py` collect 497 with no errors (7 collection errors before).
- **New end-state module**: `apps/metering/usage/tests/test_the_usage_record_is_the_posting.py` — table name, PK `id`/no sequence, `uq_posting_idempotency` (present by name and biting), the five `idx_posting_*` + `idx_posting_metadata` by final name, the inbound FK from `ubb_refund`, the neighbours (Refund, BackfillDirtyPeriod, reverse accessors), the currency column's shape and default.
- **Retired-name derivations moved to the registry** via `api.v1.tests._helpers.retired_aliases` (tests are outside the product-boundary walk): `economics.yaml` `pricing_receipt_subject_type` (both rival names), `retired.yaml` `metadata_key` (the second bag), `retired.yaml` `measurement_key` (all three aliases — wider than the one column name; checked none is an openapi property or a request field). `units` stays spelled: it is a retired SENSE, not an alias.
- **Helpers changed**: `apps/metering/usage/tests/_helpers.py` docstrings only (two `migrations/0052` → `trg_posting_measurement_released_once`; the `rule_on_the_table` "reversed-out migration" wording). `a_posting` / `committed_posting` untouched.
- **Fixture fixes beyond the brief's wording**: `_sandbox_posting` in the prune module and `_seed` in `test_analytics_scale.py` both did `Posting.objects.create` without a receipt, which now fails NOT NULL; both go through `a_posting` now. The brief said to leave `test_analytics_scale.py`'s other cases alone. I changed no case there, but `_seed` would have failed all 8 `BoundaryEquivalenceTest` cases, and the other row builders in that file were already given receipts.
- **Script**: `scripts/measure_posting_transition_cost.py` now reads the three rules (`POSTING_DECLARED_TRANSITIONS`, `POSTING_PRICE_TRANSITIONS`, `POSTING_RECEIPT_SEALING`) from `usage/0002_the_database_rules`, with uninstall taken from the same `RunSQL`'s `reverse_sql`. Every `bulk_create` row carries a v2 receipt matching its statuses; the completion batch keeps its unpriced receipt. Its old imports were stale anyway: 0039 was the pre-#557 price body, and 0040 named the pre-#370 column. I checked it without a database (`_shipped_sql` for each rule and `_a_receipt` for each batch shape) but did not run it, because it creates a database. `trg_posting_kind_frozen` stays installed in both states; this is noted in the script docstring.
- **Line endings**: the files are CRLF on disk. Files rewritten with Write were normalised back to CRLF. No BOMs.
- **Stale references outside my files (not edited)**:
  - `apps/metering/pricing/models.py:1219` (production docstring) points at `usage/tests/test_posting_rename.py`, which is now deleted. Its subject, the unowned currency-column list, was already retired by #555.
  - `apps/metering/usage/tests/test_models.py:93` says "rather than only in `test_posting_rename.py`". This should now name `test_the_usage_record_is_the_posting.py`.
  - `apps/metering/pricing/tests/test_a_rate_sits_on_the_table_named_for_a_rate.py:144` cites `usage/tests/test_posting_rename.py`.
  - `apps/metering/pricing/tests/test_a_rate_names_a_declared_quantity.py:175` cites `usage/tests/test_posting_measurement.py`'s fold replay, which is now retired.
- **Production change needed**: none. The `{}`-receipt case shows the table still admits `{}` at insert. Only `trg_posting_receipt_sealing` stops it later, and only at update. A CHECK refusing `{}` would be a production change and has not been asked for.
- **Unresolved**: none.
